          }\n\n            return base64\n                ? // Successfully registered\n            }\n            catch (ConditionalCheckFailedException)\n            {\n                return false; // Duplicate invocation\n            }\n        }\n        #endregion\n\n        #region ---------- BATCHING ----------\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Splits an <see cref=\"IEnumerable{T}\"/> into equally sized chunks.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static IEnumerable<IReadOnlyList<T>> Chunkify<T>(\n            this IEnumerable<T> source,\n            int chunkSize)\n        {\n            if (chunkSize <= 0)\n                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(chunkSize), \"Chunk size must be > 0.\");\n\n            using var enumerator = source.GetEnumerator();\n\n            while (enumerator.MoveNext())\n            {\n                var batch = new List<T>(capacity: chunkSize) { enumerator.Current };\n\n                for (int i = 1; i < chunkSize && enumerator.MoveNext(); i++)\n                    batch.Add(enumerator.Current);\n\n                yield return batch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Consumes an async sequence (or API that returns Task<IEnumerable{T}>) page-by-page\n        /// and aggregates the items into <paramref name=\"buffer\"/> until the <paramref name=\"predicate\"/> returns <c>false</c>.\n        /// A typical use-case is DynamoDB paginate-until-consumed pattern.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static async Task<List<T>> CollectWhileAsync<T>(\n            Func<string?, Task<(IReadOnlyList<T> Page, string? NextToken)>> pageLoader,\n            Predicate<T> predicate,\n            List<T>? buffer = null,\n            CancellationToken ct = default)\n        {\n            buffer ??= new List<T>();\n\n            string? token = null;\n\n            do\n            {\n                ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n\n                var (page, next) = await pageLoader(token).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                foreach (var item in page)\n                {\n                    if (!predicate(item))\n                        return buffer;\n\n                    buffer.Add(item);\n                }\n\n                token = next;\n            }\n            while (token is not null);\n\n            return buffer;\n        }\n        #endregion\n\n        #region ---------- TIME ----------\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Floors the given <see cref=\"DateTime\"/> down to the nearest minute boundary.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static DateTime FloorToMinute(this DateTime dt) =>\n            new(dt.Ticks - (dt.Ticks % TimeSpan.TicksPerMinute), DateTimeKind.Utc);\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Floors the given <see cref=\"DateTime\"/> down by <paramref name=\"quantum\"/> interval.\n        /// Commonly used to bucket temporal metrics (e.g., 5-minute focus windows).\n        /// </summary>\n        public static DateTime Floor(this DateTime dt, TimeSpan quantum)\n        {\n            if (dt.Kind != DateTimeKind.Utc)\n                dt = dt.ToUniversalTime();\n\n            long ticks = dt.Ticks / quantum.Ticks * quantum.Ticks;\n            return new DateTime(ticks, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n        }\n        #endregion\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":310,"startLine":1,"totalLines":310}}}

ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n    79→            EventEnvelope envelope,\n    80→            CancellationToken                        ct = default);\n    81→    }\n </summary>\n    91→    public sealed class KpiTransformationEngine : IAsyncDisposable\n    92→    {\n    93→        private readonly IReadOnlyList<IKpiCalculationStrategy> _strategies;\n    94→        private readonly IS3KpiWriter                           _writer;\n    95→        private readonly ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       _logger;\n    96→        private readonly SemaphoreSlim                          _parallelism;\n    97→\n    98→        public KpiTransformationEngine(\n    99→            IEnumerable<IKpiCalculationStrategy> strategies,\n   100→            IS3KpiWriter                           writer,\n   101→            ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       logger,\n   102→            int                                    maxDegreeOfParallelism = 8)\n   103→        {\n   104→            _strategies   = strategies?.ToList() ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategies));\n   105→            _writer       = writer     ?? throw new      throw new ArgumentException(\"At least one KPI strategy must be provided.\", nameof(strategies));\n   109→\n   110→            _parallelism  = new SemaphoreSlim(maxDegreeOfParallelism, public async ValueTask ExecuteAsync(\n   117→            IEnumerable<EventEnvelope> events,\n   118→            CancellationToken         ct = default)\n   119→        {\n   120→            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(events);\n   121→\n   122→            var tasks = new List<Task>();\n   123→\n   124→            foreach (var envelope in events)\n   125→            {\n   126→                await _parallelism.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   127→\n   128→                // Fire-and-forget – safe because we await all tasks in the list below\n   129→                tasks.Add(Task.Run(async () =>\n   130→                {\n   131→                    try\n   132→                    {\n   133→                        await DispatchToStrategiesAsync(envelope,                         \"Uncaught exception while processing event {EventId}. \" +\n   139→                            \"The event will be re-queued to the DLQ by the Lambda runtime.\",\n   140→                            envelope.EventId);\n   141→                        // Bubble up to trigger Lambda DLQ – do NOT swallow\n   142→                        throw;\n   143→                    }\n   144→                    finally\n   145→                    {\n   146→                        _parallelism.Release();\n   147→                    }\n   148→                }, ct));\n   149→            }\n   150→\n   151→            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   152→        }\n   153→\n   154→        private async Task DispatchToStrategiesAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n   155→        {\n   156→            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n   157→\n   158→            var interestedStrategies = _strategies.Where(s => s.CanHandle(envelope)).ToArray();\n   159→            if (interestedStrategies.Length == 0)\n   160→            {\n   161→                _logger.LogDebug(\"No KPI strategy registered for event try\n   171→                {\n   172→                    var rows = await strategy.ProcessAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n   173→                    foreach (var row in rows) 440→            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(250),\n   441→            TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(500),\n   442→            TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1),\n   443→            TimeSpan.FromSeconds(2)\n   444→        };\n   445→\n   446→        public async Task ExecuteAsync(Func<Task> operation, CancellationToken ct)\n   447→        {\n   448→            var attempt = 0;\n   449→            Exception? lastError = null;\n   450→\n   451→            foreach (var delay in BackOffSchedule.Append(TimeSpan.Zero))\n   452→            {\n   453→                try\n   454→                {\n   455→                    ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n   456→                    await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n   457→                    return; // success\n   458→                }\n   459→ InvalidOperationException(\n   469→                $\"S3 operation failed after {attempt} retries.\", lastError);\n   470→        }\n   471→\n   472→        private static bool IsTransient(Exception ex)\n   473→        {\n   474→            // TODO: Add richer fault-analysis; for now assume all AmazonS3Exception with 5xx are transient.\n   475→            return ex is AmazonS3Exception s3Ex && (int)s3Ex.StatusCode >= 500;\n   476→        }\n   477→    }\n   478→\n   479→    #endregion\n   480→}\n   481→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would The module is designed to execute inside an AWS Lambda but is consciously decoupled\n//  from the hosting environment (no direct dependency on Amazon.Lambda.* packages) so it can also be\n//  reused by Glue, Fargate, or on-prem batch jobs.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\n\nusing Amazon.S3;\nusing Amazon.S3.Model;\n\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    #region Public DTOs / Contracts -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Thin wrapper around any raw event that enters the warehouse.\n    /// Envelopes allow us to standardise metadata required for transformation,\n    /// without rewriting every single ingestion lambda when an attribute is added later on.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <remarks>\n    /// The schema mirrors the one emitted by the <c>EventIngressProcessor</c> lambda.\n    /// </remarks>\n    public sealed record EventEnvelope\n    {\n        public required string EventId          { get; parallelism, back-pressure, exception isolation, and durability (S3 write).\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiTransformationEngine : IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly IReadOnlyList<IKpiCalculationStrategy> _strategies;\n        private readonly IS3KpiWriter                           _writer;\n        private readonly ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>        /// <summary>\n        /// Orchestrates transformation for a batch of events.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async ValueTask ExecuteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<EventEnvelope> events,\n            CancellationToken         ct = default)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(events);\n\n            var tasks = new List<Task>();\n\n            foreach (var envelope in events)\n            {\n                await _parallelism.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                // Fire-and-forget – safe because we await all tasks in the list below\n                tasks.Add(Task.Run(async () =>\n                {\n                    try\n                    {\n                        await DispatchToStrategiesAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        _logger.LogError(ex,\n                            \"Uncaught exception while processing event {EventId}. \" +\n                            \"The event will be re-queued to the DLQ by the Lambda runtime.\",\n                            envelope.EventId);\n                        // Bubble up to trigger Lambda DLQ – do NOT swallow\n                        throw;\n                    }\n                    finally\n                    {\n                        _parallelism.Release();\n                    }\n                }, ct));\n            }\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private async Task DispatchToStrategiesAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n            var interestedStrategies = _strategies.Where(s => s.CanHandle(envelope)).ToArray();\n            if (interestedStrategies.Length == 0)\n            {\n                _logger.LogDebug(\"No KPI strategy registered for event {EventType}.\", envelope.EventType);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            var kpiRows = new ConcurrentBag<KpiCalculationResult>();\n\n            // Parallel but limited to #strategies so trivial overhead\n            var strategyTasks = interestedStrategies.Select(async strategy =>\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    var rows = await strategy.ProcessAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    foreach (var row in rows) kpiRows.Add(row);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"Strategy {KpiStrategy} failed while processing EventId={EventId}. Skipping strategy.\",\n                        strategy.KpiName, envelope.EventId);\n                }\n            });\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(strategyTasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            // Commit results to the Data Lake (S3, parquet files partitioned by YYYY/MM/DD)\n            if (kpiRows.Count > 0)\n            {\n                await _writer.WriteAsync(kpiRows, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n\n            _logger.LogInformation(\n                \"Processed EventId={EventId} in {ElapsedMs:n0} ms with {RowCount} KPI rows.\",\n                envelope.EventId, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds, kpiRows.Count);\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _parallelism?.Dispose();\n            if (_writer is IAsyncDisposable asyncDisposable)\n                await asyncDisposable.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Strategy Implementations ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates the ratio between focus time and total working hours on a per-user, per-day basis.\n    /// Source events:\n    ///   -           var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"OrganizerId\"]!;\n                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n\n                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n                {\n                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n                    Value          = \"meeting_minutes\"\n                    }\n                };\n\n                return new[] { result };\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Computes deployment lead-time (commit → production) on a per-repo basis.\n    /// Maintains a small in-memory buffer which is flushed every <see    _flushTimer;\n        private readonly ILogger                                _logger;\n\n        public string KpiName => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n\n        public   private void FlushExpired()\n        {\n            var threshold = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24); // assume deploy happens within 24 h\n            foreach (var kvp in _commitTimestamps)\n            {\n                if (kvp.Value < threshold && _commitTimestamps.TryRemove(kvp.Key, out _))\n                {\n                    _logger.LogDebug(\n                        \"Dropped stale commit (requests == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(requests));\n\n            var requestList = requests.ToList();\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"Processing {Count} KPI requests\", requestList.Count);\n\n            var throttler = new SemaphoreSlim(_options.DegreeOfParallelism);\n            var resultCollector = new ConcurrentBag<KpiComputationResult>();\n            var failures = new ConcurrentBag<KpiComputationFailure>();\n\n            var tasks = requestList.Select(async request =>\n            {\n                await throttler.WaitAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                try\n                {\n                    await ExecuteWithRetryAsync(request, cancellationToken, async () =>\n                    {\n                        var strategy = _strategyRegistry.Resolve(request.KpiName);\n                        _logger.LogDebug(\"Resolved strategy {Strategy} for KPI {Kpi}\",\n                            strategy.GetType().Name, request.KpiName);\n\n                        var result = await strategy.ComputeAsync(request, cancellationToken)\n                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        await PersistToLakeAsync(result, cancellationToken)\n                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        _logger.LogInformation(\"Successfully processed KPI {Kpi} for Tenant {TenantId}\",\n                            request.KpiName, request.TenantId);\n\n                        resultCollector.Add(result);\n                    }).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogError(ex, \"Failed to process KPI {Kpi} for Tenant {TenantId}\",\n                        request.KpiName, request.TenantId);\n\n                    failures.Add(new KpiComputationFailure(request, ex));\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    throttler.Release();\n                }\n            }).ToArray();\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            return new KpiBatchResult(resultCollector.ToArray(), failures.ToArray());\n        }\n\n        #region Helpers\n\n        private async Task ExecuteWithRetryAsync(\n            KpiRequest request,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken,\n            Func<Task> action)\n        {\n            var attempt = 0;\n            var delay = _options.InitialBackoff;\n\n            while (true)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    await action().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    return; // success\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransient(ex) && attempt < _options.MaxRetryAttempts)\n                {\n                    attempt++;\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"[Attempt {Attempt}/{MaxAttempts}] Transient failure while processing {Kpi} (Tenant {TenantId}). Retrying in {DelayMs}ms…\",\n                        attempt, _options.MaxRetryAttempts, request.KpiName, request.TenantId, delay.TotalMilliseconds);\n\n                    await Task.Delay(delay, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    delay = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(delay.TotalMilliseconds * _options.BackoffMultiplier);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsTransient(Exception ex)\n        {\n            return ex switch\n            {\n                AmazonServiceException ase\n                    when ase.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError or HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable => true,\n                TimeoutException => true,\n                _ => false\n            };\n        }\n\n        private async Task PersistToLakeAsync(\n            KpiComputationResult result,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n        {\n            // Example partition: kpi_name=FocusTimeRatio/year=2023/month=08/day=14/hour=13/<tenantId>.json\n            var (bucket, key) = GetS3LocationForResult(result);\n\n            using var stream = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(result).AsStream();\n            var putRequest = new PutObjectRequest\n            {\n                BucketName = bucket,\n                Key = key,\n                InputStream = stream,\n                ContentType = \"application/json\",\n                AutoCloseStream = true\n            };\n\n            await _s3Client.PutObjectAsync(putRequest, cancellationToken)\n                .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Persisted KPI {Kpi} result to s3://{Bucket}/{Key}\", result.KpiName, bucket, key);\n        }\n\n        private (string Bucket, string Key) GetS3LocationForResult(KpiComputationResult result)\n        {\n            var timestamp = result.GeneratedAtUtc;\n            var key =\n                $\"{result.KpiName}/year={timestamp:yyyy}/month={timestamp:MM}/day={timestamp:dd}/hour={timestamp:HH}/{result.TenantId}.json\";\n\n            return (_options.S3Bucket, key);\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n    }\n\n    #region Options & Contracts\n\n    public sealed record OrchestratorOptions(\n        int DegreeOfParallelism,\n        int MaxRetryAttempts,\n        TimeSpan              double.TryParse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name), out var value) ? value : defaultValue;\n\n            string GetString(string name, string defaultValue) =>\n                Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name) ?? defaultValue;\n\n            return new OrchestratorOptions(\n                DegreeOfParallelism: GetInt(\"KPI_DOP\", Environment.ProcessorCount),\n                MaxRetryAttempts: GetInt(\"KPI_MAX_RETRIES\", 3),\n                InitialBackoff: TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(GetInt(\"KPI_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS\", 400)),\n                BackoffMultiplier: It embraces the Observer pattern in order to be\n// embeddable in both streaming (push) and batch (pull) scenarios while\n// still preserving functional purity through a user-supplied\n// aggregation delegate.\n//\n// Key capabilities\n// • Tumbling & sliding windows                                             \n// • Thread-safe, lock-free hot path                                        \n// • Back-pressure aware via System.Threading.Channels                      \n// • Real-time Observable<KpiMetric> output                                 \n// • Fault-tolerant (bubble-up strategy / DLQ compatible)                   \n// ---------------------------------------------\n#nullable enable\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Globalization;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Reactive.Linq;\nusing System.Reactive.Subjects;\nusing System.Runtime.CompilerServices;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Channels;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Single domain event coming from any ingestion source.\n    /// A deliberately flat structure that can be re-shaped downstream.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <param name=\"Timestamp\">UTC timestamp the event occurred.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"EventType\">Human-friendly event discriminator.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"ActorId\">Primary user / system identifier.</param>\n    /// <param name=\"NumericValue\">\n    /// Optional raw measurement (e.g. duration seconds, bytes, …).\n    /// When null the <see cref=\"Attributes\"/> collection must carry meaning.\n    /// </param>\n    /// <param name=\"Attributes\">Catch-all property bag.</param>\n    public sealed record ProductivityEvent(\n        DateTimeOffset Timestamp,\n        string EventType,\n        string ActorId,\n        decimal? </summary>\n    public sealed class TimeSeriesWindowAggregator : IObserver<ProductivityEvent>, IObservable<KpiMetric>, IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly KpiAggregationDelegate _aggregationLogic;\n        private readonly TimeWindowOptions _options;\n\n        private readonly Subject<KpiMetric> _metricStream = new();\n        private readonly Channel<ProductivityEvent> _inputChannel;\n\n        // Concurrency primitives\n        private readonly CancellationTokenSource _cts = new();\n        private readonly Task _backgroundTask;\n\n        // Internal buffer storing events inside the current active window(s)\n        // We deliberately use a ConcurrentQueue to keep insertion O(1) and avoid\n        // global locks on the hot path.\n        private readonly ConcurrentQueue<ProductivityEvent> _buffer = new();\n\n        // Latest watermark processed (monotonic)\n        private DateTimeOffset _currentWindowCutoff;\n\n        public TimeSeriesWindowAggregator(\n            KpiAggregationDelegate aggregationLogic,\n            TimeWindowOptions options,\n            int boundedCapacity = 10_000) // backpressure\n        {\n            options.Validate();\n\n            _aggregationLogic = aggregationLogic ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(aggregationLogic));\n            _options = options;\n\n            _inputChannel = Channel.CreateBounded<ProductivityEvent>(new BoundedChannelOptions(boundedCapacity)\n            {\n                SingleReader = true,\n                SingleWriter = false,\n                FullMode = BoundedChannelFullMode.Wait\n            });\n\n            _currentWindowCutoff = AlignToWindowStart(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);\n\n            _backgroundTask = Task.Factory.StartNew(\n                WorkLoopAsync,\n                _cts.Token,\n                TaskCreationOptions.LongRunning,\n                TaskScheduler.Default).Unwrap();\n        }\n\n        #region IObserver\n\n boundedChannel Wait\n                throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Input channel rejected write unexpectedly.\");\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void OnError(Exception error)\n        {\n            // Bubble up to downstream subscribers – they'll decide what to do.\n            _metricStream.OnError(error);\n        }\n\n        public void OnCompleted()\n        {\n            _inputChannel.Writer.Complete();\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n\n        #region IObservable\n\n        public IDisposable Subscribe(IObserver<KpiMetric> observer)\n        {\n            return _metricStream.AsObservable().Subscribe(observer);\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n\n        private async Task WorkLoopAsync()\n        {\n            try\n            {\n                var reader = _inputChannel.Reader;\n\n                while (await reader.WaitToReadAsync(_cts.Token).ConfigureAwait(false))\n                {\n                    while (reader.TryRead(out var evt))\n                    {\n                        _buffer.Enqueue(evt);\n                    }\n\n                    FlushExpiredWindows();\n                }\n            }\n            catch (OperationCanceledException)\n            {\n                // Normal shutdown – ignore.\n            }\n            catch (Exception ex)\n            {\n                // Surface unexpected exceptions so ops team is alerted (PagerDuty).\n                _metricStream.OnError(ex);\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                _metricStream.OnCompleted();\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Remove records that no longer belong to any open window and emit\n        /// completed windows downstream.\n        /// </summary>\n        [MethodImpl(MethodImplOptions.AggressiveInlining)]\n        private void FlushExpiredWindows()\n        {\n            var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;\n\n            // Move watermark forward in increments of SlideInterval until the current time is covered.\n            while (_currentWindowCutoff + _options.WindowSize <= now)\n            {\n                var windowStart = _currentWindowCutoff;\n                var windowEnd = windowStart + _options.WindowSize;\n\n                // Gather relevant events.\n                var windowEvents = DrainEvents(windowStart, windowEnd);\n\n                // Apply user-supplied aggregation delegate.\n                try\n                {\n                    foreach (var metric in _aggregationLogic(windowEvents, windowStart, windowEnd))\n                    {\n                        _metricStream.OnNext(metric);\n                    }\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    // Fail-fast on faulty business logic so we don't silently produce wrong numbers.\n                    _metricStream.OnError(new AggregationException(\n                        $\"Aggregation delegate threw for window {windowStart:o}-{windowEnd:o}\", ex));\n                }\n\n                // Advance watermark\n                _currentWindowCutoff = windowStart + _options.SlideInterval;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Retrieve and remove events from the buffer that are part of the\n        /// requested window interval.\n        /// </summary>\n        private IReadOnlyCollection<ProductivityEvent> DrainEvents(DateTimeOffset windowStart, DateTimeOffset windowEnd)\n        {\n            var set = new List<ProductivityEvent>(capacity: 1024);\n\n            while (_buffer.TryPeek(out var candidate))\n            {\n                if (candidate.Timestamp < windowStart)\n                {\n                    // Too old: discard\n                    _buffer.TryDequeue(out _);\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                if (candidate.Timestamp >= windowEnd)\n                {\n                    // Still in future window – stop draining.\n                    break;\n                }\n\n                // In window range\n                _buffer.TryDequeue(out var inWindow);\n                if (inWindow != null)\n                {\n                    set.Add(inWindow);\n                }\n            }\n\n            return set;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Aligns the given timestamp to the previous aligned window boundary.\n        /// </summary>\n        private DateTimeOffset AlignToWindowStart(DateTimeOffset timestamp)\n        {\n            var ticks = (long)(_options.SlideInterval.Ticks *\n                               Math.Floor(timestamp.UtcTicks / (double)_options.SlideInterval.Ticks));\n            return new DateTimeOffset(ticks, TimeSpan.Zero);\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _cts.Cancel();\n\n            // Wait for graceful termination, but don't block forever.\n            var completedTask = await Task.WhenAny(_backgroundTask, Task.Delay(5000)).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            if (completedTask != _backgroundTask)\n            {\n                // Force task to fault for diagnostic purposes\n                _metricStream.OnError(new TimeoutException(\"Aggregator failed to shut down in timely manner.\"));\n            }\n\n            _cts.Dispose();\n            _metricStream.Dispose();\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Aggregation delegate convenience builder that computes a simple\n        /// sum over NumericValue field.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static KpiAggregationDelegate SumNumeric(string metricName, Func<ProductivityEvent, bool>? predicate = null)\n            => (events, start, end) =>\n            {\n                var query = predicate is null ? events : events.Where(predicate);\n                var total = query.Sum(e => e.NumericValue.GetValueOrDefault());\n                return new[]\n                {\n                    new KpiMetric(start, end, metricName, total, DimensionKeys: null)\n                };\n            };\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Bundles aggregation related failures so they can be handled explicitly\n    /// by an upstream error boundary (e.g. Lambda DLQ).\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class AggregationException : Exception\n    {\n        public AggregationException(string message, Exception innerException)\n            : base(message, innerException)\n        {\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Example usage wiring in a Lambda context\n    //\n    // var options = new TimeWindowOptions\n    // {\n    //     WindowSize = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5),\n    //     SlideInterval = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(1)\n    // };\n If both denominators are zero, value is <c>double.NaN</c>.\n   149→    /// </summary>\n   150→    public sealed class FocusTimeRatioStrategy : IKpiComputationStrategy\n   151→    {\n   152→        public const string Identifier = \"focus_time_ratio\";\n   153→\n   154→        public string KpiName => Identifier;\n   155→\n   156→        /// <inheritdoc />\n   157→        public async Task<KpiResult> ComputeAsync(\n   158→            KpiComputationContext context,\n   159→            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n   160→        {\n   161→            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(context);\n   162→\n   163→            var logger = context.Logger ?? The engine is used by several\n// Lambda micro-components (stream, batch, CDC) to convert raw telemetry into\n// domain KPIs.  Strategies are resolved through attribute-based discovery and\n// wired via Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection so that teams can\n// hot-swap business logic without re-deploying the outer Lambda shell.\n//\n// AWS SSM is used for real-time feature-flag control, enabling Ops to promote\n// or roll back strategies instantly.  The engine is thread-safe, highly\n// observable, and defensive against poisoned messages.\n//\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Reflection;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Amazon.SimpleSystemsManagement;\nusing Amazon.SimpleSystemsManagement.Model;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    #region Public Contracts\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///   DTO for an incoming pipeline record.  The envelope contains both the\n    ///   raw JSON payload and a set of normalized headers used throughout the\n    ///   warehouse mesh.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed record RecordEnvelope(\n        string Stream,               // e.g. “prod/github/push”\n        string SchemaVersion,        // e.g. “v1”\n        DateTimeOffset Timestamp,    // producer timestamp\n        JsonDocument Payload)        // opaque JSON blob\n    {\n        public string TenantId =>\n            Payload.RootElement.TryGetProperty(\"tenantId\", out var t)\n                ? t.GetString() ?? \"unknown\"\n                : \"unknown\";\n\n        /// <summary>Convenience accessor for correlation id.</summary>\n        public string CorrelationId =>\n            Payload.RootElement.TryGetProperty(\"correlationId\", out var c)\n                ? c.GetString() ??        {\n            var strategyTypes =\n                Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly()\n                        .DefinedTypes\n                        .Where(t => !t.IsAbstract\n                                    && typeof(IDataTransformationStrategy).IsAssignableFrom(t)\n                                    && t.GetCustomAttribute<TransformationStrategyAttribute>() is not null)\n                        .ToArray();\n\n            foreach (var type in strategyTypes)\n            {\n                services.AddSingleton(typeof(IDataTransformationStrategy), type);\n                bootstrapLogger?.LogInformation(\n                    \"Registered transformation strategy {StrategyType}\", type.FullName);\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        ///   Entry tex)\n            {\n                // Business-level exception: send to DLQ with context\n                _logger.LogWarning(tex,\n                    \"Transformation failed (tenant={Tenant}, Strategy Resolution Internals\n\n        private async Task<IDataTransformationStrategy> ResolveStrategyAsync(\n            string tenant,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n        {\n            var (strategyName, _) =\n                await GetTenantStrategyNameAsync(tenant, cancellationToken);\n\n            if (_strategyCache.TryGetValue(strategyName, out var strategy))\n                      .FirstOrDefault(s =>\n                          s.Name.Equals(strategyName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));\n\n            if (strategy is null)\n            {\n strategyName);\n\n                strategy = new NoopTransformationStrategy();\n            }\n\n            _strategyCache[strategyName] = strategy;\n            return strategy;\n        }\n\n        private async Task<(string StrategyName, DateTimeOffset Expires)>\n            GetTenantStrategyNameAsync(string tenant, CancellationToken token)\n        {\n            if WithDecryption = false\n                    }, token);\n\n                entry = (response.Parameter.Value, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow + _parameterTtl);\n            }\n            catch tenant);\n\n            return entry;\n        }\n\n        #endregion\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Built-in Strategies\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///   Default no-op strategy that simply Task.FromResult(envelope.Payload.RootElement.Clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    ///   Calculates Meeting Cost Index (MCI) based on calendar telemetry.\n    ///   The resulting JSON adheres to the KPI v1 schema.\n    /// </summary>\n    [TransformationStrategy(\"MeetingCostIndex\")]\n    internal sealed class MeetingCostIndexStrategy : IDataTransformationStrategy\n    {\n        public string Name => \"MeetingCostIndex\";\n\n        public async Task<JsonElement> TransformAsync(\n            RecordEnvelope envelope,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            // Simulate I/O latency for demonstration purposes\n            await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(15), cancellationToken);\n\n            var root = envelope.Payload.RootElement;\n\n            if (!root.TryGetProperty(\"attendees\", out var attendees))\n                throw new TransformationException(\"Payload missing 'attendees' property\");\n\n            if (!root.TryGetProperty(\"durationMinutes\", out var durationProp)\n                || !durationProp.TryGetInt32(out var durationMinutes))\n                throw new TransformationException(\"Payload missing or invalid 'durationMinutes'\");\n\n            var totalHourlyCost = attendees.EnumerateArray()\n                                           .Select(a => a.GetProperty(\"hourlyRateUsd\").GetDecimal())\n                                           .Sum();\n\n            var cost = totalHourlyCost * (durationMinutes / 60m);\n\n            using var doc = Acts as a façade for discovery, selection, and execution.\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class TransformationEngine\n    {\n        private readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<string, KpiResult>> RunAsync(\n            IAsyncEnumerable<RawEvent> events,\n            IEnumerable<string> strategyNames,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n           .ToList();\n\n            var missing = uniqueNames.Where(n => !_strategies.ContainsKey(n)).ToList();\n            if (missing.Any())\n                throw new ArgumentException($\"Unknown Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n                // Copy the buffer into an async stream for each strategy.\n                async IAsyncEnumerable<RawEvent> Stream()\n                {\n                    foreach (var ev in buffer)\n                    {\n                        ct.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                        yield return ev;\n                    }\n                }\n\n                var result = await strategy.TransformAsync(Stream(), ct);\n                _logger.LogDebug(\"KPI strategy {Name} completed in {ElapsedMs}ms.\", name, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds);\n                return result;\n            }\n            catch (OperationCanceledException)\n            {\n                _logger.LogWarning(\"KPI strategy {Name} canceled.\", name);\n                throw;\n Intended to centralise defensive checks.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <param name=\"key\">Name of the environment variable to resolve.</param>\n        /// <returns>Resolved environment variable value.</returns>\n        /// <exception cref=\"InvalidOperationException\">\n        ///     Thrown when the environment variable is missing or empty.\n        /// </exception>\n        public static string RequireEnv(string key)\n        {\n            var value = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(key);\n            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))\n            {\n                throw new InvalidOperationException($\"Required environment variable '{key}' is missing or empty.\");\n            }\n\n            return value;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        ///     Convenience overload that returns <paramref name=\"fallback\"/> when the variable\n        ///     is not set, avoiding an exception for non-critical defaults.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static string GetEnv(string key, string fallback) =>\n            string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(key))\n                ? fallback\n                : Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(key)!;\n\n        /// <summary>\n        ///     Converts the stage (dev|staging|prod) string into a strongly-typed enum.\n        ///     Defensive by design: an unrecognised stage will raise an exception early.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static DeploymentStage GetDeploymentStage()\n        {\n            var stage = RequireEnv(Env.Stage).ToLowerInvariant();\n            return stage switch\n            {\n                \"dev\"     => DeploymentStage.Dev,\n                \"staging\" => DeploymentStage.Staging,\n                \"prod\"    => DeploymentStage.Prod,\n                _ => throw new InvalidOperationException(\n                    $\"Unsupported deployment stage '{stage}'.   /// </summary>\n   159→        public static string ComputeDeterministicHash(ReadOnlySpan<byte> payload, bool base64 = true)\n   160→        {\n   161→            Span<byte> hash      = stackalloc          Span<byte> hash      = stackalloc byte[32]; // SHA-256\n            bool rented          = false;\n            Span<byte> buffer    = payload;\n\n            if (!payload.TryCopyTo(hash)) // i < chunkSize && enumerator.MoveNext(); i++)\n                    batch.Add(enumerator.Current);\n\n                yield return batch;\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Consumes an async sequence (or API that returns Task<IEnumerable{T}>) page-by-page\n        /// and aggregates the items into <paramref name=\"buffer\"/> until the <paramref name=\"predicate\"/> returns <c>false</c>.\n        /// A typical use-case is DynamoDB paginate-until-consumed pattern.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static async Task<List<T>> CollectWhileAsync<T>(\n            Func<string?, Task<(IReadOnlyList<T> Page, string? NextToken)>> pageLoader,\n            Predicate<T> predicate,\n            List<T>? buffer = null,\n            CancellationToken ct = default)\n        {\n            buffer ??= new List<T>();\n\n quantum.Ticks * quantum.Ticks;\n            return new DateTime(ticks, DateTimeKind.Utc);\n        }\n        #endregion\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Centralised resilience helper for public static class ResilienceHelper\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes an asynchronous operation with exponential backoff retry strategy.\n        /// The retry policy is configured via <see cref=\"PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_MAX_RETRIES\"/>\n        /// and <see cref=\"PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS\"/>.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <typeparam name=\"T\">The return type of the asynchronous operation.</typeparam>\n        /// <param name=\"operation\">The asynchronous operation to execute with retry semantics.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Optional cancellation token for cooperative cancellation.</param>\n        /// <returns>The result of the asynchronous operation.</returns>\n        /// <exception cref=\"ArgumentNullException\">Thrown when <paramref name=\"operation\"/> is null.</exception>\n        /// <exception cref=\"Exception\">Re-throws the last exception if all retry attempts fail.</exception>\n        /// <remarks>\n        /// The method implements exponential backoff where the delay between attempts doubles\n        /// with each retry: delay = API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS * 2^attempt (0-indexed).\n        /// Transient exceptions (HttpRequestException, TimeoutException, WebException, and\n        /// AmazonServiceException with 5xx status codes) are retried up to API_MAX_RETRIES times.\n        /// </remarks>\n        public static async Task<T> ExecuteWithRetryAsync<T>(\n            Func<Task<T>> operation,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (operation == null)\n                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(operation));\n\n            var maxRetries = PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_MAX_RETRIES;\n            var initialBackoffMs = PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS;\n\n            Exception? lastException = null;\n\n            for (int attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    return await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n                {\n                    // Cancellation requested — do not retry, propagate immediately\n                    throw;\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransientException(ex) && attempt < maxRetries)\n                {\n                    lastException = ex;\n\n                    // Calculate exponential backoff delay: initialBackoffMs * 2^attempt\n                    var delayMs = initialBackoffMs * (int)Math.Pow(2, attempt);\n                    await Task.Delay(delayMs, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    // Non-transient exception or final attempt — propagate\n                    lastException = ex;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n\n            // All retries exhausted — re-throw the last exception\n            throw lastException!;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes an asynchronous operation (no return value) with exponential backoff retry strategy.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <param name=\"operation\">The asynchronous operation to execute with retry semantics.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Optional cancellation token for cooperative cancellation.</param>\n        /// <exception cref=\"ArgumentNullException\">Thrown when <paramref name=\"operation\"/> is null.</exception>\n        /// <exception cref=\"Exception\">Re-throws the last exception if all retry attempts fail.</exception>\n        public static async Task ExecuteWithRetryAsync(\n            Func<Task> operation,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (operation == null)\n                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(operation));\n\n            // Wrap the void-returning operation in a Task<bool> and delegate to the generic method\n            await ExecuteWithRetryAsync(async () =>\n            {\n                await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                return true;\n            }, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Determines whether an exception is considered transient and should be retried.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <param name=\"exception\">The exception to evaluate.</param>\n        /// <returns><c>true</c> if the exception is transient; otherwise, <c>false</c>.</returns>\n        private static bool TimeoutException => true,\n                WebException => true,\n                AmazonServiceException ase when\n                    ase.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.BadGateway\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.GatewayTimeout\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests => true,\n                _ => false\n            };\n        }\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//  Description\n//  ------------\n//  Transformation module that materialises productivity-oriented KPIs from a heterogenous event feed.\n//  Implements the Strategy-Pattern so that additional KPIs can be plugged in without redeploying the\n//  compute units.  The module is designed to execute inside an AWS Lambda but is consciously decoupled\n//  from the hosting environment (no direct dependency on Amazon.Lambda.* packages) so it can also be\n//  reused by Glue, Fargate, or on-prem batch jobs.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Diagnostics;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\n\nusing Amazon.S3;\nusing Amazon.S3.Model;\n\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    #region Public DTOs / Contracts -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Thin wrapper around any raw event that enters the warehouse.\n    /// Envelopes allow us to standardise metadata required for transformation,\n    /// without rewriting every single ingestion lambda when an attribute is added later on.\n    /// </summary>\n    /// <remarks>\n    /// The schema mirrors the one emitted by the <c>EventIngressProcessor</c> lambda.\n    /// </remarks>\n    public sealed record EventEnvelope\n    {\n        public required string EventId          { get; parallelism, back-pressure, exception isolation, and durability (S3 write).\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiTransformationEngine : IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        private readonly IReadOnlyList<IKpiCalculationStrategy> _strategies;\n        private readonly IS3KpiWriter                           _writer;\n        private readonly ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       _logger;\n        private readonly SemaphoreSlim                          _parallelism;\n\n        public KpiTransformationEngine(\n            IEnumerable<IKpiCalculationStrategy> strategies,\n            IS3KpiWriter                           writer,\n            ILogger<KpiTransformationEngine>       logger,\n            int                                    maxDegreeOfParallelism = 8)\n        {\n            _strategies   = strategies?.ToList() ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategies));\n            _writer       = writer     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(writer));\n            _logger       = logger     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n            if (!_strategies.Any())\n                throw new ArgumentException(\"At least one KPI strategy must be provided.\", nameof(strategies));\n\n            _parallelism  = new SemaphoreSlim(maxDegreeOfParallelism, maxDegreeOfParallelism);\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Orchestrates transformation for a batch of events.\n        /// </summary>\n        public async ValueTask ExecuteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<EventEnvelope> events,\n            CancellationToken         ct = default)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(events);\n\n            var tasks = new List<Task>();\n\n            foreach (var envelope in events)\n            {\n                await _parallelism.WaitAsync(ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                // Fire-and-forget – safe because we await all tasks in the list below\n                tasks.Add(Task.Run(async () =>\n                {\n                    try\n                    {\n                        await DispatchToStrategiesAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    }\n                    catch (Exception ex)\n                    {\n                        _logger.LogError(ex,\n                            \"Uncaught exception while processing event {EventId}. \" +\n                            \"The event will be re-queued to the DLQ by the Lambda runtime.\",\n                            envelope.EventId);\n                        // Bubble up to trigger Lambda DLQ – do NOT swallow\n                        throw;\n                    }\n                    finally\n                    {\n                        _parallelism.Release();\n                    }\n                }, ct));\n            }\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private async Task DispatchToStrategiesAsync(EventEnvelope envelope, CancellationToken ct)\n        {\n            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();\n\n            var interestedStrategies = _strategies.Where(s => s.CanHandle(envelope)).ToArray();\n            if (interestedStrategies.Length == 0)\n            {\n                _logger.LogDebug(\"No KPI strategy registered for event {EventType}.\", envelope.EventType);\n                return;\n            }\n\n            var kpiRows = new ConcurrentBag<KpiCalculationResult>();\n\n            // Parallel but limited to #strategies so trivial overhead\n            var strategyTasks = interestedStrategies.Select(async strategy =>\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    var rows = await strategy.ProcessAsync(envelope, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    foreach (var row in rows) kpiRows.Add(row);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"Strategy {KpiStrategy} failed while processing EventId={EventId}. Skipping strategy.\",\n                        strategy.KpiName, envelope.EventId);\n                }\n            });\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(strategyTasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            // Commit results to the Data Lake (S3, parquet files partitioned by YYYY/MM/DD)\n            if (kpiRows.Count > 0)\n            {\n                await _writer.WriteAsync(kpiRows, ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n            }\n\n            _logger.LogInformation(\n                \"Processed EventId={EventId} in {ElapsedMs:n0} ms with {RowCount} KPI rows.\",\n                envelope.EventId, sw.ElapsedMilliseconds, kpiRows.Count);\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask DisposeAsync()\n        {\n            _parallelism?.Dispose();\n            if (_writer is IAsyncDisposable asyncDisposable)\n                await asyncDisposable.DisposeAsync().ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Strategy Implementations ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Calculates the ratio between focus time and total working hours on a per-user, per-day basis.\n    /// Source events:\n    ///   - CalendarEventCreated / Updated\n    ///   - WorkSessionStarted / Ended (emitted by IDE plugins, CLI wrappers, browser extensions, …)\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class FocusTimeRatioStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy\n    {\n        private const string CalendarEventType = \"CalendarEvent\";\n        private const string WorkSessionType   = \"WorkSession\";\n\n        public string KpiName => \"focus_time_ratio\";\n\n        public bool CanHandle(EventEnvelope envelope)\n            => envelope.EventType is CalendarEventType or WorkSessionType;\n\n        public async ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n            EventEnvelope   envelope,\n            CancellationToken ct = default)\n        {\n            // NOTE: In a real-world scenario, we would use a distributed cache (e.g., DAX or Redis)\n            //       to store partial aggregates.  For brevity, this implementation simply returns\n            //       an immediate KPI row when sufficient information is present on the event itself.\n\n            if (envelope.EventType == WorkSessionType &&\n                envelope.Metadata.TryGetValue(\"DurationMinutes\", out var durationStr) &&\n                int.TryParse(durationStr, out var durationMinutes))\n            {\n                var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"UserId\"]!;\n                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n\n                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n                {\n                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n                    Value          = durationMinutes, // later aggregated to obtain total focus time\n                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n                        [\"metric\"]  = \"focus_minutes\"\n                    }\n                };\n\n                return new[] { result };\n            }\n\n            if (envelope.EventType == CalendarEventType &&\n                envelope.Metadata.TryGetValue(\"DurationMinutes\", out var calendarDurationStr) &&\n                int.TryParse(calendarDurationStr, out var meetingMinutes))\n            {\n                var userId = envelope.Metadata[\"OrganizerId\"]!;\n                var date   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime.Date;\n\n                var result = new KpiCalculationResult\n                {\n                    KpiName        = KpiName,\n                    WindowStartUtc = date,\n                    WindowEndUtc   = date.AddDays(1),\n                    Value          = meetingMinutes,\n                    Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                    {\n                        [\"user_id\"] = userId,\n                        [\"metric\"]  = \"meeting_minutes\"\n                    }\n                };\n\n                return new[] { result };\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Computes deployment lead-time (commit → production) on a per-repo basis.\n    /// Maintains a small in-memory buffer which is flushed every <see cref=\"FlushInterval\"/>.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy : IKpiCalculationStrategy, IDisposable\n    {\n        private const string CommitEventType     = \"CodeCommitPushed\";\n        private const string ProdDeployEventType = \"CdPipelineDeployed\";\n\n        private readonly TimeSpan                               FlushInterval     = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);\n        private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, DateTime> _commitTimestamps = new(StringComparer.Ordinal);\n        private readonly Timer                                  _flushTimer;\n        private readonly ILogger                                _logger;\n\n        public string KpiName => \"deployment_lead_time\";\n\n        public DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy(ILogger<DeploymentLeadTimeStrategy> logger)\n        {\n            _logger      = logger;\n            _flushTimer  = new Timer(_ => FlushExpired(), null, FlushInterval, FlushInterval);\n        }\n\n        public bool CanHandle(EventEnvelope envelope)\n            => envelope.EventType is CommitEventType or ProdDeployEventType;\n\n        public async ValueTask<IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult>> ProcessAsync(\n            EventEnvelope envelope,\n            CancellationToken ct = default)\n        {\n            switch (envelope.EventType)\n            {\n                case CommitEventType:\n                    {\n                        var commitSha  = envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n                        _commitTimestamps[commitSha] = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime;\n                        break;\n                    }\n\n                case ProdDeployEventType:\n                    {\n                        var commitSha = envelope.Metadata[\"CommitSha\"]!;\n                        if (_commitTimestamps.TryRemove(commitSha, out var commitTime))\n                        {\n                            var leadTimeMinutes = (envelope.Created.UtcDateTime - commitTime).TotalMinutes;\n                            var repo            = envelope.Metadata[\"Repository\"]!;\n\n                            var kpiRow = new KpiCalculationResult\n                            {\n                                KpiName        = KpiName,\n                                WindowStartUtc = commitTime,\n                                WindowEndUtc   = envelope.Created.UtcDateTime,\n                                Value          = (decimal)leadTimeMinutes,\n                                Dimensions = new Dictionary<string, string>\n                                {\n                                    [\"repository\"] = repo\n                                }\n                            };\n                            return new[] { kpiRow };\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    }\n            }\n\n            return Array.Empty<KpiCalculationResult>();\n        }\n\n        private void FlushExpired()\n        {\n            var threshold = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(-24); // assume deploy happens within 24 h\n            foreach (var kvp in _commitTimestamps)\n            {\n                if (kvp.Value < threshold && _commitTimestamps.TryRemove(kvp.Key, out _))\n                {\n                    _logger.LogDebug(\n                        \"Dropped stale commit {CommitSha} for deployment lead-time KPI (older than 24h).\",\n                        kvp.Key);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        public void Dispose()\n        {\n            _flushTimer.Dispose();\n        }\n    }\n\n    #endregion\n\n    #region Persistence Layer -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Simple abstraction that serialises KPI rows as ND-JSON and stores them in S3.\n    /// Real-world implementation could write Apache Parquet or Iceberg; ND-JSON keeps demo lightweight.\n    /// </summary>\n    public interface IS3KpiWriter : IAsyncDisposable\n    {\n        ValueTask WriteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult> rows,\n            CancellationToken                 ct = default);\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Production-ready S3 writer implementation with retry/back-off and partitioned key structure.\n    /// </summary>\n    internal sealed class S3KpiWriter : IS3KpiWriter\n    {\n        private readonly IAmazonS3                 _s3;\n        private readonly string                    _bucket;\n        private readonly ILogger<S3KpiWriter>      _logger;\n\n        private static readonly AmazonS3RetryPolicy RetryPolicy = new();\n\n        public S3KpiWriter(IAmazonS3 s3, string bucket, ILogger<S3KpiWriter> logger)\n        {\n            _s3     = s3     ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(s3));\n            _bucket = bucket ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(bucket));\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n        }\n\n        public async ValueTask WriteAsync(\n            IEnumerable<KpiCalculationResult> rows,\n            CancellationToken                 ct = default)\n        {\n            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(rows);\n\n            var nowUtc     = DateTime.UtcNow;\n            var partition  = $\"{nowUtc:yyyy/MM/dd}\";\n            var objectKey  = $\"kpi/partition_date={partition}/{Guid.NewGuid():N}.jsonl\";\n\n            // Serialise as ND-JSON\n            var jsonLines  = string.Join('\\n', rows.Select(row => System.Text.Json.JsonSerializer.Serialize(row)));\n\n            var request = new PutObjectRequest\n            {\n                BucketName  = _bucket,\n                Key         = objectKey,\n                ContentBody = jsonLines,\n                ContentType = \"application/json\"\n            };\n\n            await RetryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(\n                async () => await _s3.PutObjectAsync(request, ct).ConfigureAwait(false),\n                ct).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Wrote {RowCount} KPI rows to s3://{Bucket}/{Key}.\", rows.Count(), _bucket, objectKey);\n        }\n\n        public ValueTask DisposeAsync() => ValueTask.CompletedTask;\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Declarative, exponential back-off retry wrapper for S3 Intelligence Fabric\n//\n// Description:\n//  Implements the Strategy-Pattern based KPI computation orchestrator that runs\n//  inside a Lambda.  The orchestrator resolves a concrete IKpiComputationStrategy\n//  for the requested KPI, fans the work out across multiple threads (or\n//  reserved-concurrency Lambda instances, depending on runtime settings),\n//  handles transient failures with an exponential back-off policy, and finally\n//  streams the normalized result back to the Data-Lake.\n//\n//  The class is intentionally generic and can be reused from both the\n//  stream-processing (real-time) and batch-processing (historical back-fill)\n//  pipelines.\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Concurrent;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing System.Linq;\nusing System.Net;\nusing System.Text.Json;\nusing System.Threading;\nusing System.Threading.Tasks;\nusing Amazon;\nusing Amazon.Runtime;\nusing Amazon.S3;\nusing Amazon.S3.Model;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;\nusing PulseOps.Warehouse.Common;\nusing PulseOps.Warehouse.Contracts;\nusing PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations.Strategies;\n\nnamespace PulseOps.Warehouse.Transformations\n{\n    /// <summary>\n    /// Orchestrates KPI computations by delegating the heavy lifting to a pool of\n    /// <see cref=\"IKpiComputationStrategy\"/> implementations.  Responsibilities:\n    ///\n    /// • Resolve the correct strategy for a given <see cref=\"KpiRequest\"/>\n    /// • Run the computation across a configurable degree of parallelism\n    /// • Persist results to the Lake (S3) using a structured partition scheme\n    /// • Emit CloudWatch observable metrics & traces\n    /// • Provide robust error-handling / back-off semantics\n    /// </summary>\n    public sealed class KpiComputationOrchestrator : IKpiComputationOrchestrator\n    {\n        private const string DefaultRegion = \"us-east-1\";\n        private readonly IKpiComputationStrategyRegistry _strategyRegistry;\n        private readonly IAmazonS3 _s3Client;\n        private readonly ILogger<KpiComputationOrchestrator> _logger;\n        private readonly OrchestratorOptions _options;\n\n        public KpiComputationOrchestrator(\n            IKpiComputationStrategyRegistry strategyRegistry,\n            ILogger<KpiComputationOrchestrator> logger,\n            OrchestratorOptions? options = null,\n            IAmazonS3? s3Client = null)\n        {\n            _strategyRegistry = strategyRegistry ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(strategyRegistry));\n            _logger = logger ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(logger));\n            _options = options ?? OrchestratorOptions.FromEnvironment();\n            _s3Client = s3Client ?? new AmazonS3Client(FallbackCredentialsFactory.GetCredentials(), RegionEndpoint.GetBySystemName(DefaultRegion));\n        }\n\n        public async Task<KpiBatchResult> ProcessBatchAsync(\n            IEnumerable<KpiRequest> requests,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (requests == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(requests));\n\n            var requestList = requests.ToList();\n            _logger.LogInformation(\"Processing {Count} KPI requests\", requestList.Count);\n\n            var throttler = new SemaphoreSlim(_options.DegreeOfParallelism);\n            var resultCollector = new ConcurrentBag<KpiComputationResult>();\n            var failures = new ConcurrentBag<KpiComputationFailure>();\n\n            var tasks = requestList.Select(async request =>\n            {\n                await throttler.WaitAsync(cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                try\n                {\n                    await ExecuteWithRetryAsync(request, cancellationToken, async () =>\n                    {\n                        var strategy = _strategyRegistry.Resolve(request.KpiName);\n                        _logger.LogDebug(\"Resolved strategy {Strategy} for KPI {Kpi}\",\n .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        await PersistToLakeAsync(result, cancellationToken)\n                            .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n                        _logger.LogInformation(\"Successfully processed KPI {Kpi} for Tenant {TenantId}\",\n                            request.KpiName, request.TenantId);\n\n                        resultCollector.Add(result);\n                    }).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    _logger.LogError(ex, \"Failed to process KPI {Kpi} for Tenant {TenantId}\",\n                        request.KpiName, request.TenantId);\n\n                    failures.Add(new KpiComputationFailure(request, ex));\n                }\n                finally\n                {\n                    throttler.Release();\n                }\n            }).ToArray();\n\n            await Task.WhenAll(tasks).ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            return new KpiBatchResult(resultCollector.ToArray(), failures.ToArray());\n        }\n\n        #region Helpers\n\n        private async Task ExecuteWithRetryAsync(\n            KpiRequest request,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken,\n            Func<Task> action)\n        {\n            var attempt = 0;\n            var delay = _options.InitialBackoff;\n\n            while (true)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    await action().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    return; // success\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransient(ex) && attempt < _options.MaxRetryAttempts)\n                {\n                    attempt++;\n                    _logger.LogWarning(ex,\n                        \"[Attempt {Attempt}/{MaxAttempts}] Transient failure while processing {Kpi} (Tenant {TenantId}). cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                    delay = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(delay.TotalMilliseconds * _options.BackoffMultiplier);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsTransient(Exception ex)\n        {\n            return ex switch\n            {\n                AmazonServiceException ase\n                    when ase.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError or HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable => true,\n                TimeoutException => true,\n                _ => false\n            };\n        }\n\n        private async Task PersistToLakeAsync(\n            KpiComputationResult result,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken)\n        {\n            // Example partition: kpi_name=FocusTimeRatio/year=2023/month=08/day=14/hour=13/<tenantId>.json\n            var (bucket, key) = GetS3LocationForResult(result);\n\n            using var stream = JsonSerializer.SerializeToUtf8Bytes(result).AsStream();\n            var putRequest = new PutObjectRequest\n            {\n                BucketName = bucket,\n                Key = key,\n                InputStream = stream,\n                ContentType = \"application/json\",\n                AutoCloseStream = true\n            };\n\n            await _s3Client.PutObjectAsync(putRequest, cancellationToken)\n                .ConfigureAwait(false);\n\n            _logger.LogDebug(\"Persisted KPI {Kpi} result to s3://{Bucket}/{Key}\", result.KpiName, bucket, key);\n        }\n\n        private (string Bucket, string Key) GetS3LocationForResult(KpiComputationResult result)\n        {\n            var timestamp = result.GeneratedAtUtc;\n            var key =\n                $\"{result.KpiName}/year={timestamp:yyyy}/month={timestamp:MM}/day={timestamp:dd}/hour={timestamp:HH}/{result.TenantId}.json\";\n\n            return (_options.S3Bucket, key);\n        }\n\n              double.TryParse(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name), out var value) ? value : defaultValue;\n\n            string GetString(string name, string defaultValue) =>\n                Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(name) ?? defaultValue;\n\n            return new OrchestratorOptions(\n                DegreeOfParallelism: GetInt(\"KPI_DOP\", Environment.ProcessorCount),\n _logger.LogDebug(\"Computed Meeting-Cost Index {Cost} for Tenant {Tenant}\", costIndex, request.TenantId);\n\n            return new KpiComputationResult(\n                TenantId: public static class ResilienceHelper\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes an asynchronous operation with exponential backoff retry strategy.\n        /// The retry policy is configured via <see cref=\"PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_MAX_RETRIES\"/>\n        /// and <see cref=\"PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS\"/>.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <typeparam name=\"T\">The return type of the asynchronous operation.</typeparam>\n        /// <param name=\"operation\">The asynchronous operation to execute with retry semantics.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Optional cancellation token for cooperative cancellation.</param>\n        /// <returns>The result of the asynchronous operation.</returns>\n        public static async Task<T> ExecuteWithRetryAsync<T>(\n            Func<Task<T>> operation,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (operation == null)\n                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(operation));\n\n            var maxRetries = PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_MAX_RETRIES;\n            var initialBackoffMs = PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS;\n\n            Exception? lastException = null;\n\n            for (int attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    return await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n                {\n                    throw;\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransientException(ex) && attempt < maxRetries)\n                {\n                    lastException = ex;\n                    var delayMs = initialBackoffMs * (int)Math.Pow(2, attempt);\n                    await Task.Delay(delayMs, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    lastException = ex;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n\n            throw lastException!;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes an asynchronous operation (no return value) with exponential backoff retry strategy.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static async Task ExecuteWithRetryAsync(\n            Func<Task> operation,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (operation == null)\n                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(operation));\n\n            await ExecuteWithRetryAsync(async () =>\n            {\n                await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                return true;\n            }, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsTransientException(Exception exception)\n        {\n            return exception switch\n            {\n                System.Net.Http.HttpRequestException => true,\n                TimeoutException => true,\n                WebException => true,\n                AmazonServiceException ase when\n                    ase.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.BadGateway\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.GatewayTimeout\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests => true,\n                _ => false\n            };\n        }\n    }\n```\n\n### 3. public static class ResilienceHelper\n    {\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes an asynchronous operation with exponential backoff retry strategy.\n        /// The retry policy is configured via <see cref=\"PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_MAX_RETRIES\"/>\n        /// and <see cref=\"PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS\"/>.\n        /// </summary>\n        /// <typeparam name=\"T\">The return type of the asynchronous operation.</typeparam>\n        /// <param name=\"operation\">The asynchronous operation to execute with retry semantics.</param>\n        /// <param name=\"cancellationToken\">Optional cancellation token for cooperative cancellation.</param>\n        /// <returns>The result of the asynchronous operation.</returns>\n        public static async Task<T> ExecuteWithRetryAsync<T>(\n            Func<Task<T>> operation,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (operation == null)\n                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(operation));\n\n            var maxRetries = PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_MAX_RETRIES;\n            var initialBackoffMs = PulseOps.Warehouse.Shared.PulseOpsConstants.API_INITIAL_BACKOFF_MS;\n\n            Exception? lastException = null;\n\n            for (int attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++)\n            {\n                try\n                {\n                    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested();\n                    return await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (OperationCanceledException) when (cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested)\n                {\n                    throw;\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex) when (IsTransientException(ex) && attempt < maxRetries)\n                {\n                    lastException = ex;\n                    var delayMs = initialBackoffMs * (int)Math.Pow(2, attempt);\n                    await Task.Delay(delayMs, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n                }\n                catch (Exception ex)\n                {\n                    lastException = ex;\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n\n            throw lastException!;\n        }\n\n        /// <summary>\n        /// Executes an asynchronous operation (no return value) with exponential backoff retry strategy.\n        /// </summary>\n        public static async Task ExecuteWithRetryAsync(\n            Func<Task> operation,\n            CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)\n        {\n            if (operation == null)\n                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(operation));\n\n            await ExecuteWithRetryAsync(async () =>\n            {\n                await operation().ConfigureAwait(false);\n                return true;\n            }, cancellationToken).ConfigureAwait(false);\n        }\n\n        private static bool IsTransientException(Exception exception)\n        {\n            return exception switch\n            {\n                System.Net.Http.HttpRequestException => true,\n                TimeoutException => true,\n                WebException => true,\n                AmazonServiceException ase when\n                    ase.StatusCode is HttpStatusCode.InternalServerError\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.BadGateway\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.GatewayTimeout\n                                   or HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests => true,\n                _ => false\n            };\n        }\n    }\n```\n\n### 3.
